============================================================================\n\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3&      minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Subdivide into 8 children\n        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;\n        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];\n\n        for (size_t idx : indices) {\n            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];\n\n            // Check which children this triangle overlaps\n            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n                Vec3 childCenter = center;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\n\n                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\n                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() Vec3 childMax = center;\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\n                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\n                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\n                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : childMax);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\npublic:\n    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\n        Vec3 d = (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n   std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// boxCenter;\n\n    // Triangle edges\n    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;\n    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;\n\n    // Test box normals (AABB axes)\n    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {\n        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\n    };\n\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\n\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\n        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);\n    };\n\n    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};\n    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};\n    for (const auto& axis : axes) {\n        for (const auto& edge : edges) {\n            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);\n            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {\n                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Octree for Spatial Acceleration\n// ============================================================================\n\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\n\nclass Octree {\npublic:\n    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;\n    Vec3 halfExtent, center;\n    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];\n    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list\n    const small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n Each rank owns a contiguous block of triangles (rows) and never\n//   stores the full matrices, so memory scales with 1/P.\n// * The row blocks are balanced by the number of non-culled pairs per row,\n//   which is exactly proportional to the form-factor work of that row.\n// * The serial program draws all form-factor Monte-Carlo samples from one\n//   mt19937 stream in row-major order. A non-culled pair consumes exactly\n//   4*NUM_RAYS draws, a culled pair consumes none. Each rank therefore\n//   positions its own generator at the exact stream offset where its row\n//   block begins (fast O(log n) Mersenne-Twister jump-ahead for large\n//   offsets, discard() for small ones). We obtain\n// p(x) at runtime with Berlekamp-Massey on an output bit sequence, compute\n// g(x) = x^(n-1) mod p(x) by square-and-multiply, evaluate g(M)*state with a\n// Horner scheme on the 624-word window, and then apply one final real step\n// (this last step makes the 31 \"dead\" bits of the first word exact as well).\n// Every polynomial is bit-packed in 64-bit words.\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace mtjump {\n\nconstexpr int MT_N = 624;\nconstexpr int MT_M = 397;\nconstexpr uint32_t MATRIX_A = 0x9908b0dfU;\nconstexpr uint32_t UPPER_MASK = 0x80000000U;\nconstexpr uint32_t LOWER_MASK = 0x7fffffffU;\nconstexpr int DEG = 19937;                 // degree of the minimal polynomial\nconstexpr int PW = (DEG + 1 + 63) / 64;    // words holding a degree-DEG polynomial\nconstexpr int NSEQ = 2 * DEG;              // sequence length needed by Berlekamp-Massey\n\nusing Poly = std::vector<uint64_t>;\n\ninline uint32_t stepWord(uint32_t a, uint32_t b, uint32_t c) {\n    uint32_t y = (a & UPPER_MASK) | (b & LOWER_MASK);\n    return c ^ (y >> 1) ^ ((y & 1u) ? }\ninline void setBit(Poly& p, size_t b) { p[b >> 6] |= (uint64_t{1} << (b & 63)); }\n\n// dst ^= (src[0..nsrc) << shift); dst must be large enough.\ninline void xorShifted(Poly& dst, const Poly& src, size_t nsrc, size_t shift) {\n    size_t ws = shift >> 6;\n    unsigned bs = static_cast<unsigned>(shift & 63);\n    if (bs == 0) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < DEG; ++i) {\n        if (getBit(r, static_cast<size_t>(i))) setBit(tmp, static_cast<size_t>(2 * i));\n    }\n    // Reduce from degree 2*(DEG-1) down to DEG\n    for (int b = 2 * (DEG - 1); b >= DEG; i < MT_N; ++i) acc[(k + i) % MT_N] ^= w[i];\n        }\n    }\n    // Final real step: acc = M^n * w exactly (including dead bits)\n    acc[k] = stepWord(acc[k], acc[(k + 1) % MT_N], acc[(k + MT_M) % MT_N]);\n    k = (k + 1) % MT_N;\n    for (int i = 0; i < MT_N; ++i) out[i] = acc[(k + i) % MT_N];\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Set 'rng' to the state of std::mt19937(seed) advanced by n outputs.\ninline bool jump(std::mt19937& rng, uint32_t seed, uint64_t n) {\n    uint32_t win[MT_N];\n    if (!jumpWindow(seed, n, win)) return false;\n    // Textual state representation: x_{i-n} ... x_{i-1} followed by the index.\n    std::ostringstream os;\n    for (int i = 0; i < MT_N; ++i) os << win[i] << ' ';\n    os << MT_N;\n    std::istringstream is(os.str());\n    is >> rng;\n    return static_cast<bool>(is);\n}\n\n}  // namespace mtjump\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\n    uint32_t seed_;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f), seed_(seed) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); r < g_size; ++r) {\n        size_t s;\n        if (total == 0) {\n            s = (n * static_cast<size_t>(r)) / static_cast<size_t>(g_size);\n        } else {\n            // Target: r/P of the total weight. MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Collective: every rank must call it; the result is identical on all ranks.\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n    bool allFinite = true;\n\n    for (size_t i = break;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    // Count non-zero local form factors (global count via reduction)\n    long long localNonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.kij.size(); ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) localNonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;\n    MPI_Allreduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (!allFinite) return false;\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Distance ROOT_PRINTF(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if       100.0f * static_cast<val_t>(nonZeroKij) / static_cast<val_t>(totalPairs));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        ROOT_PRINTF(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per       subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n//     timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0 && i + 1 == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\n\");\n\n    // Precomputation\n    double startPre = phaseTimerStart();\n\n    computeTimeDelays(state);\n    computeFormFactors(state);\n\n    long preDuration = phaseTimerStopMs(startPre);\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"Precomputation time: %ld ms\\n\", preDuration);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\n\");\n\n    // Simulation\n    double startSim = phaseTimerStart();\n\n    runSimulation(state);\n\n    long simDuration = phaseTimerStopMs(startSim);\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", simDuration);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\n\");\n\n    // Distance computation\n    double startDist = phaseTimerStart();\n\n    computeDistances(state);\n\n    long distDuration = phaseTimerStopMs(startDist);\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"Distance computation time: %ld ms\\n\", distDuration);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\n\");\n\n    // Total time\n    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"Total computation time: %ld ms\\n\", totalTime);\n\n    // Performance metrics\n    size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;\n    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);\n    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);\n    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\n\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\nPerformance:\\n\");\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Triangles: %zu\\n\", n);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Timesteps: %zu\\n\", t);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Form factor computations: %.2e\\n\", kijOps);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Simulation operations: %.2e\\n\", simOps);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Distance computations: %.2e\\n\", distOps);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\\n\", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);\n\n    // Memory usage (aggregate over all ranks for the distributed matrices)\n    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);\n    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);\n    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);\n    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Memory usage: %.2f MB\\n\", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n\n    // Hash\n    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n    ROOT_PRINTF(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && g_rank == 0) {\n        // Convert distances to double for {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgoDA3pLimf4Wj1tUTB4H","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The MPI parallelization of roomsim is complete, verified bit-identical to the original, and scales to about 117x on 128 cores.\n\n**What changed** (only `roomsim/roomsim.cpp` and `roomsim/CMakeLists.txt`, executable still `roomsim`):\n\n- **Row-distributed matrices.** Each rank owns a contiguous block of triangles and stores only its rows of the N×N form-factor and time-delay matrices. Memory per rank scales as 1/P. Blocks are balanced by the number of non-culled pairs per row, which is exactly proportional to the form-factor work.\n- **Exact reproduction of the serial RNG stream.** The original draws all Monte-Carlo samples from one `mt19937` in row-major order. Every non-culled pair consumes 64 draws and culled pairs consume none, so each rank's stream offset is known from a prefix sum. Small offsets use `discard`. Large offsets use a Mersenne Twister jump-ahead I implemented in the `mtjump` namespace: Berlekamp–Massey recovers the degree-19937 minimal polynomial at startup, square-and-multiply computes the jump polynomial, and a Horner scheme applies it to the 624-word state. A jump takes under 100 ms for any offset, versus up to 9 s for `discard` at the largest mesh. It falls back to `discard` if anything looks wrong.\n- **Wave propagation** advances one timestep at a time. Each rank computes its rows and the row is shared with an in-place `MPI_Allgatherv`. Time delays are always at least one step, so no intra-step dependency exists. Summation order per row is unchanged.\n- **Distances** are computed for local rows and allgathered. Validation is collective, with the non-zero form-factor count reduced across ranks. Only rank 0 prints, and phase timers are barrier-synchronized.\n- **CMake** uses `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`. Build is warning-free.\n\n**Verification.** Result hashes, the `-o` output block, and validation output match the serial program exactly at 20, 80, 320, 1280, and 5120 triangles across 1 to 256 ranks, including a run with more ranks than triangles and a run with non-default `-t`, `-s`, `-r`.\n\n**Performance** (AMD EPYC, 128 physical cores, total computation time):\n\n| Triangles | Serial | 64 ranks | 128 ranks | 256 ranks (HT) |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1280 | 26.6 s | 0.47 s | 0.25 s | 0.27 s |\n| 5120 | 471 s | 8.2 s | 4.0 s | 3.7 s |\n\nMeasured per-row cost is uniform within 2%, so the static weighted partition stays well balanced even at 256 ranks. One caveat for the reader: the jump-ahead loads the state through the stream operators, which I verified against `discard` with libstdc++; it is guarded by a fallback should another standard library format the state differently."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":380,"cache_read_input_tokens":94651,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":380},"output_tokens":754,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d93d5644-952a-476c-a094-b28d0c1cf984","uuid":"2cccebaf-5b19-4bc1-8ece-411d1d6b7c02","timestamp":"2026-11-07T07:56:51.442Z","request_id":"req_011CgoDA3dAhJDY7RjNmj4zX"}
